# Win-probability model (batch 9, 133 matches)

Every number is win-chance points for an otherwise even game (50%), learned from the matches so far; "+-" is the uncertainty across bags. Few matches = rough curves.

## How well it predicts (matches held out whole)

**Verdict: beats strength alone (not yet clear: the range includes 0).** Team strength alone is the baseline; the curves below are what the game state adds to it.

Gain over strength alone (log loss, resampling whole matches): +0.086, 90% range -0.035 to +0.196.

- strength only: accuracy 76%, log loss 0.463 (0-5 min 81%; 5-10 min 79%; 10-15 min 72%; 15-end min 66%; matches lasting 15 min+ 70%; matches even on strength (gap under 200) 57%)
- glass box: accuracy 84%, log loss 0.377 (0-5 min 84%; 5-10 min 85%; 10-15 min 81%; 15-end min 87%; matches lasting 15 min+ 82%; matches even on strength (gap under 200) 72%)
- glass box without corrections: accuracy 80%, log loss 0.493 (0-5 min 79%; 5-10 min 80%; 10-15 min 76%; 15-end min 84%; matches lasting 15 min+ 76%; matches even on strength (gap under 200) 65%)
- black box: accuracy 77%, log loss 0.809 (0-5 min 80%; 5-10 min 78%; 10-15 min 74%; 15-end min 72%; matches lasting 15 min+ 72%; matches even on strength (gap under 200) 64%)

## Corrections applied

- lord_damage_diff & minute: interaction (Denning (agreeing with Claude's batch 3 finding): Lord damage only decides a match at 28:00 (the tiebreak); before that a team can deal a lot of Lord damage and still lose to a Lord kill, so Lord damage should be read together with the clock.)
- lord_dps_own: cap (Denning (2026-11-07, agreeing with Claude's batch 7 finding): each team's Lord damage per second is fixed by its build, so it is one value per team per match; the curves zigzagged in mirror image, each step resting on 11 to 14 team-matches with no trend in win rate (memorizing which teams won). Held-out log loss on 91 matches: as before 0.404, both dropped 0.413, directions 0.393, cap at 5 points 0.377 (best), cap + directions 0.384.)
- lord_dps_enemy: cap (Denning (2026-11-07, agreeing with Claude's batch 7 finding): each team's Lord damage per second is fixed by its build, so it is one value per team per match; the curves zigzagged in mirror image, each step resting on 11 to 14 team-matches with no trend in win rate (memorizing which teams won). Held-out log loss on 91 matches: as before 0.404, both dropped 0.413, directions 0.393, cap at 5 points 0.377 (best), cap + directions 0.384.)
- healing_monks_own: drop (Denning (2026-11-07, agreeing with Claude's batch 8 finding): healing monks are one value per team per match and almost every team runs 3 (0 monks: 1 team-match, 4 monks: 5), so the curves' extreme steps rest on almost nothing and zigzag in mirror image. Held-out log loss on 113 matches: as before 0.385, cap at 5 points 0.382, dropped 0.367 (best).)
- healing_monks_enemy: drop (Denning (2026-11-07, agreeing with Claude's batch 8 finding): healing monks are one value per team per match and almost every team runs 3 (0 monks: 1 team-match, 4 monks: 5), so the curves' extreme steps rest on almost nothing and zigzag in mirror image. Held-out log loss on 113 matches: as before 0.385, cap at 5 points 0.382, dropped 0.367 (best).)
